      review/check-final-integrity.py
 1 """Independent consistency checks for the frozen handoff evidence.
 2
 3 Hash verification is an integrity check, not a substitute for numerical review.
 4 Final review/repro/acceptance are deliberately outside their own hash closure.
 5 """
 6 from pathlib import Path
 7 import hashlib, json

 9 ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
10 def read(p): return json.loads((ROOT / p).read_text())
11 def sha(p): return hashlib.sha256((ROOT / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
12 def check_hashes(items):
13    for name, expected in items.items():
14        assert sha(name) == expected, name
15    return len(items)

17 def main():
18    checks = {}
19    registry = read('artifacts/result-registry.json')['results']

24              part = part.replace('~1', '/').replace('~0', '~')

26         assert row['status'] == 'verified' and row['value'] == obj, row['id']
27    checks['registry_exact_source_values'] = {'passed': True, 'records': len(registry)}

49    assert not production['pending_cases'] and not production['failed_cases']
50    supplement = read('review/final-supplement.json')

               ,→ interval headers.',
64              'Workbook SOC in kWh denotes energy inventory, as explicitly distinguished from the normalized SOC
               ,→ ratio in the model document.',

67                'passed': True, 'scope': 'frozen_evidence_consistency_and_sampled_visual_review',

51        assert max(0,float(np.max(n+c-d-a['q'])))<1e-6
52        return {'passed':True,'economic_cost_yuan':exact['objective_cost_yuan'],'energy_kwh':float(a['q'].sum()),

54                'normalization_simultaneous_max':float(np.minimum(c,d).max()),
55                'files':{f:sha(ROOT/f) for f in ['artifacts/q1.json','artifacts/q1.npz']}}

      review/check-global-oracle.py
 1 """Independent full-horizon perfect-information LP, not an executable policy.
 2
 3 Run from the project directory: python3 review/check-global-oracle.py
 4 Four LPs use all 48,096 actual ten-minute intervals, no daily closure, no
 5 emergency energy, no regularization term, and explicit free spill variables.
 6 """
 7 from pathlib import Path
 8 import hashlib, json, platform, sys, time
 9 import numpy as np
10 from scipy.sparse import eye, diags, hstack, vstack, csr_matrix
11
12 ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
13 sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'vendor'))
14 import highspy
15
16 ETA=.9; M=5000./6.; EMIN=1200.; EMAX=10800.; INITIAL=6000.
17 OUTPUT=ROOT/'review'
18
19 def sha(path):return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
20
21 def matrices(T):
22       I=eye(T,format='csr'); Z=csr_matrix((T,T))
23       D=I-diags(np.ones(T-1),-1,shape=(T,T),format='csr')
24       # Column blocks q, c, d, next E, spill.
25       return vstack([hstack([I,-I,I,Z,-I]),hstack([Z,-ETA*I,I/ETA,D,Z])],format='csr')
26




                                                              75
27 def one_case(A,net,price,terminal,label):
28    begin=time.perf_counter();T=len(net);N=5*T
29    objective=np.r_[price,np.zeros(4*T)]
30    lo=np.r_[np.zeros(3*T),np.full(T,EMIN),np.zeros(T)]
31    hi=np.r_[np.full(T,np.inf),np.full(2*T,M),np.full(T,EMAX),np.full(T,np.inf)]
32    if terminal is not None:lo[4*T-1]=hi[4*T-1]=terminal
33    rhs=np.r_[net,np.r_[INITIAL,np.zeros(T-1)]]
34    problem=highspy.HighsLp();problem.num_col_=N;problem.num_row_=2*T
35    problem.col_cost_=objective;problem.col_lower_=lo;problem.col_upper_=hi
36    problem.row_lower_=rhs;problem.row_upper_=rhs
37    problem.a_matrix_.format_=highspy.MatrixFormat.kRowwise
38    problem.a_matrix_.start_=A.indptr;problem.a_matrix_.index_=A.indices;problem.a_matrix_.value_=A.data
39    solver=highspy.Highs()
40    options={'output_flag':False,'threads':1,'solver':'ipm',
41             'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8,'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8,
42             'ipm_optimality_tolerance':1e-10}
43    for key,value in options.items():
44        assert solver.setOptionValue(key,value)==highspy.HighsStatus.kOk
45    assert solver.passModel(problem)==highspy.HighsStatus.kOk
46    status=solver.run();model_status=solver.getModelStatus()
47    assert status==highspy.HighsStatus.kOk,status
48    assert model_status==highspy.HighsModelStatus.kOptimal,solver.modelStatusToString(model_status)
49    sol=solver.getSolution();info=solver.getInfo()
50    assert sol.value_valid and sol.dual_valid
51    x=np.asarray(sol.col_value);y=np.asarray(sol.row_dual);z=np.asarray(sol.col_dual)
52    q,c,d,E,spill=np.split(x,5)
53    cost=float(price@q);raw_objective=float(solver.getObjectiveValue())
54    finite=np.isfinite(hi)
55    # Solver reduced-cost dual objective, with infeasible infinite-bound signs
56    # reported separately. An independently repaired certificate follows.
57    raw_dual=float(rhs@y+np.sum(lo*np.maximum(z,0))+
58                   np.sum(hi[finite]*np.minimum(z[finite],0)))
59    stationarity=float(abs(objective-A.T@y-z).max())
60    infinite_sign_violation=float(max(0,-z[~finite].min()))
61
62    # For unbounded-above q and spill, dual feasibility is exactly 0<=y_bus<=p.
63    # All other columns have finite bounds and need no reduced-cost sign limit.
64    # Clipping these rows constructs a feasible dual directly, independently
65    # recomputing every reduced cost rather than relying on the solver report.
66    certificate_y=y.copy();certificate_y[:T]=np.clip(certificate_y[:T],0,price)
67    certificate_z=objective-A.T@certificate_y
68    assert certificate_z[~finite].min()>=-1e-12
69    dual_bound=float(rhs@certificate_y+np.sum(lo*np.maximum(certificate_z,0))+
70                     np.sum(hi[finite]*np.minimum(certificate_z[finite],0)))
71    gap=cost-dual_bound
72    relative_gap=gap/max(1,abs(cost))
73    eq_residual=float(abs(A@x-rhs).max())
74    bounds_residual=float(max(0,np.max(lo-x),np.max(x[finite]-hi[finite])))
75
76    # Any simultaneous charge/discharge is removed while keeping E and q.
77    # Extra bus supply is sent to the explicit free spill variable.
78    simultaneous_before=float(np.minimum(c,d).max())
79    remove=np.minimum(c,d/(ETA*ETA))
80    canonical_c=c-remove;canonical_d=d-ETA*ETA*remove
81    canonical_spill=spill+(1-ETA*ETA)*remove
82    state=np.r_[INITIAL,E]
83    balance_error=float(abs(q+canonical_d-canonical_c-canonical_spill-net).max())
84    soc_error=float(abs(np.diff(state)-ETA*canonical_c+canonical_d/ETA).max())
85    power=max(canonical_c.max(),canonical_d.max())*6
86    simultaneous_after=float(np.minimum(canonical_c,canonical_d).max())
87    midnight=state[np.arange(144,T+1,144)]
88    end_error=0. if terminal is None else abs(float(state[-1])-terminal)
89    checks={'matrix_equality_max_abs':eq_residual,'variable_bound_violation':bounds_residual,
90            'economic_objective_recompute_error':abs(cost-raw_objective),
91            'dual_stationarity_max_abs':stationarity,'raw_infinite_bound_sign_violation':infinite_sign_violation,
92            'dual_certificate_repair_max_abs':float(abs(certificate_y-y).max()),
93            'dual_certificate_unbounded_column_violation':float(max(0,-certificate_z[~finite].min())),
94            'primal_minus_repaired_dual':gap,'relative_primal_dual_gap':relative_gap,
95            'canonical_bus_balance_max_abs':balance_error,'canonical_soc_recurrence_max_abs':soc_error,
96            'canonical_soc_min':float(state.min()),'canonical_soc_max':float(state.max()),



                                                         76
97             'canonical_power_max_kw':float(power),'simultaneous_before':simultaneous_before,
98             'simultaneous_after':simultaneous_after,'terminal_error':end_error}
99     assert max(eq_residual,bounds_residual,balance_error,soc_error,end_error)<1e-5,checks
100    assert state.min()>=EMIN-1e-5 and state.max()<=EMAX+1e-5 and power<=5000+1e-5
101    assert min(q.min(),canonical_c.min(),canonical_d.min(),canonical_spill.min())>=-1e-5
102    assert abs(gap)<max(.01,abs(cost)*1e-8) and stationarity<1e-6,checks
103    result={'label':label,'status':solver.modelStatusToString(model_status),'terminal_constraint':terminal,
104            'objective_cost_yuan':cost,'solver_objective_yuan':raw_objective,
105            'raw_dual_objective_yuan':raw_dual,'repaired_dual_lower_bound_yuan':dual_bound,
106            'initial_inventory_kwh':INITIAL,'final_inventory_kwh':float(state[-1]),
107            'ordinary_purchase_kwh':float(q.sum()),'spill_kwh':float(canonical_spill.sum()),
108            'charge_bus_kwh':float(canonical_c.sum()),'discharge_bus_kwh':float(canonical_d.sum()),
109            'midnight_inventories_kwh':midnight.tolist(),
110            'checks':checks,'iterations':{'ipm':int(info.ipm_iteration_count),
111                                         'crossover':int(info.crossover_iteration_count),
112                                         'simplex':int(info.simplex_iteration_count)},
113            'runtime_seconds':time.perf_counter()-begin,'solver_options':options,
114            'solver_report':{'max_primal_infeasibility':float(info.max_primal_infeasibility),
115                             'max_dual_infeasibility':float(info.max_dual_infeasibility)}}
116    arrays={'q':q,'c':canonical_c,'d':canonical_d,'state':state,'spill':canonical_spill}
117    return result,arrays

119 def main():
120    start=time.perf_counter();source=sha(__file__);data_path=ROOT/'artifacts/data.npz';input_hash=sha(data_path)
121    data=dict(np.load(data_path));net=((data['load'][31:]-data['pv'][31:])/6).ravel()
122    T=len(net);assert T==48096
123    prices={'fixed':np.tile(data['day_price'],334),'variable':data['price'][31:].ravel()}
124    assert all(p.min()>0 and len(p)==T for p in prices.values())
125    A=matrices(T);results={};trajectories={}
126    for kind,price in prices.items():
127         for name,terminal in [('free',None),('fixed6000',6000.)]:
128              label=f'{kind}_{name}';print('Solving '+label,flush=True)
129              result,arrays=one_case(A,net,price,terminal,label)
130              results[label]=result
131              trajectories.update({f'{label}_{k}':v for k,v in arrays.items()})
132              print(json.dumps({'label':label,'cost':result['objective_cost_yuan'],
133                                'gap':result['checks']['primal_minus_repaired_dual'],
134                                'seconds':result['runtime_seconds']}),flush=True)
135    for kind in prices:
136         assert results[f'{kind}_free']['objective_cost_yuan']<=results[f'{kind}_fixed6000']['objective_cost_yuan' ⌋
           ,→ ]+1e-5
137    archive=OUTPUT/'global-oracle-trajectories.npz'
138    np.savez_compressed(archive,**trajectories)
139    assert source==sha(__file__) and input_hash==sha(data_path)
140    report={'schema_version':1,'reviewer_id':'/root/independent_review','independent':True,
141              'scope':'Full-horizon perfect-information optimistic reference, not a causal policy or SDDP gap.',
142              'passed':True,'evaluation':{'start_date':str(data['dates'][31]),'end_date':str(data['dates'][-1]),
143                                         'days':334,'ten_minute_intervals':T,'initial_inventory_kwh':INITIAL,
144                                         'daily_closure':False,'emergency_purchase_allowed':False,
145                                         'free_spill':True,'turnover_regularizer':0.,
146                                         'eta_each_direction':ETA,'power_kw':5000,'soc_bounds_kwh':[EMIN,EMAX]},
147              'lower_bound_argument':'For any feasible causal strategy with identical initial and true terminal
                ,→ conditions, use perfect-information ordinary purchase q_oracle=r+emergency with its battery and
                ,→ spill. phi(q,r)>=r and 5*emergency>=emergency, so positive-price cost cannot increase. Removing
                ,→ nonanticipativity therefore gives a lower bound. The free-terminal bound must not be presented as
                ,→ a same-terminal gap for a fixed-terminal policy.',
148              'dual_certificate':'Clip the bus-balance row dual to [0,price], recompute z=c-A^T y, and evaluate b^T
                ,→ y + sum(z_positive*lower) + sum(z_negative*finite_upper). All infinite-upper columns are q and
                ,→ spill and have nonnegative reduced costs after repair. Numerical tolerances are explicitly
                ,→ reported; this is not exact rational arithmetic.',
149              'results':results,'source_sha256':source,'data_npz_sha256':input_hash,
150              'trajectory_file':str(archive.relative_to(ROOT)),'trajectory_sha256':sha(archive),
151              'execution':{'command':'python3 review/check-global-oracle.py','exit_code':0,
152                           'runtime_seconds':time.perf_counter()-start,'python':sys.version,
153                           'numpy':np.__version__,'highs':highspy.Highs().version(),'platform':platform.platform(),
154                           'deterministic_reason':'Four deterministic sparse LPs; no sampling or random seed.'}}
155    (OUTPUT/'global-oracle.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n')
156    print(json.dumps({'passed':True,'total_seconds':report['execution']['runtime_seconds']}),flush=True)

     review/check-production.py
 1 """Independent audit of 36 full production trajectories; no solver/model calls.
 2
 3 python3 review/check-production.py                       # partial live inventory
 4 python3 review/check-production.py --require-complete    # final numerical gate
 5
 6 Energy, costs, physics, release reconstruction, and calendar rules below are
 7 implemented independently with NumPy. No production module is imported.
 8 Prior code/mutation/LP reviews are identified as reused evidence, not rerun here.
 9 """
10 from pathlib import Path
11 import hashlib, json, sys, time
12 import numpy as np
13
14 ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
15 OUT=ROOT/'review/production-audit.json'
16 ETA=.9; M=5000/6; EMIN=1200.; EMAX=10800.; INITIAL=6000.
17 DAYS=np.arange(31,365); DATES=np.arange(np.datetime64('2025-02-01'),np.datetime64('2026-01-01'))
18 TEND=365*144; TOL=1e-5
19 KINDS=['q2','q3','q4_2','q4_3']
20 METHODS=['closed_baseline','cross_baseline','affine_mpc','markov_mpc','closed_affine']
21 ABLATIONS={'0only':[0],'without6':[0,2,3],'without12':[0,1,3],'without18':[0,1,2]}
22 DATA=dict(np.load(ROOT/'artifacts/data.npz'))
23 MODEL_CACHE={}
24
25 class Pending(Exception):pass
26 def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
27 def require(test,message):
28       if not bool(test):raise AssertionError(message)
29 def near(a,b,label,tol=TOL):
30      aa=np.asarray(a);bb=np.asarray(b)
31      err=float(np.max(np.abs(aa-bb))) if aa.size else 0.
32      require(np.isfinite(err) and err<=tol,f'{label}: {err}')
33      return err
34 def read_pair(stem):
35      j=ROOT/(stem+'.json');n=ROOT/(stem+'.npz')
36      if not j.exists() or not n.exists():raise Pending('Missing '+', '.join(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for p in [j,n]
       ,→ if not p.exists()))
37      before={str(p.relative_to(ROOT)):sha(p) for p in [j,n]}
38      try:
39          meta=json.loads(j.read_text());arrays=dict(np.load(n))
40      except (ValueError,EOFError,OSError) as e:raise Pending('Incomplete/unreadable pair: '+str(e))
41      after={str(p.relative_to(ROOT)):sha(p) for p in [j,n]}
42      if before!=after:raise Pending('Files changed during read')
43      return arrays,meta,after
44
45 def daily_ledger(a):
46        q,r,p,e=a['q'],a['r'],a['price'],a['emergency']
47        ledger={'planned_cost':np.sum(p*q,axis=1),
48                'increase_cost':np.sum(1.5*p*np.maximum(r-q,0),axis=1),
49                'reduction_net_cost':np.sum(-.5*p*np.maximum(q-r,0),axis=1),
50                'emergency_cost':np.sum(5*p*e,axis=1),
51                'emergency_energy':e.sum(1),'spill_energy':a['spill'].sum(1),
52                'projection_count':np.sum(a['projection']>1e-6,axis=1)}
53        ledger['total_cost']=sum(ledger[k] for k in
         ,→ ['planned_cost','increase_cost','reduction_net_cost','emergency_cost'])
54        near(ledger['total_cost'],np.sum(p*(np.maximum(1.5*r-.5*q,.5*r+.5*q)+5*e),axis=1),'two independent fee forms')
55        return ledger
56
57 def physical(a,kind,initial,terminal=None,closed=False):
58       nd=len(a['days']);days=np.asarray(a['days'])
59       for key in ['q','r','c','d','emergency','spill','price','projection']:
60           require(a[key].shape==(nd,144),key+' shape')
61           require(np.isfinite(a[key]).all(),key+' finite')
62       require(a['state'].shape==(nd,145),'state shape')



                                                             78
63      require(np.isfinite(a['state']).all(),'state finite')
64      near(a['dates'].astype('datetime64[D]').astype(int),DATA['dates'][days].astype('datetime64[D]').astype(int),' ⌋
       ,→ data date mapping',0)
65      q,r,c,d,e,w,E=[a[k] for k in ['q','r','c','d','emergency','spill','state']]
66      net=(DATA['load'][days]-DATA['pv'][days])/6
67      prices=DATA['price'][days] if kind.startswith('q4') else np.broadcast_to(DATA['day_price'],(nd,144))
68      near(a['price'],prices,'actual settlement price',0)
69      nonnegative=float(max(0,-min(a[k].min() for k in ['q','r','c','d','emergency','spill','projection'])))
70      require(nonnegative<=TOL,'negative physical energy')
71      result={'balance_max_abs':near(r+e+d-c-w,net,'bus balance'),
72              'soc_recurrence_max_abs':near(np.diff(E,axis=1),ETA*c-d/ETA,'SOC recurrence'),
73              'midnight_jump_max_abs':near(E[1:,0],E[:-1,-1],'midnight continuity'),
74              'soc_min':float(E.min()),'soc_max':float(E.max()),
75              'power_max_kw':float(max(c.max(),d.max())*6),
76              'simultaneous_max':near(np.minimum(c,d),0,'simultaneous charge/discharge'),
77              'initial_inventory':float(E[0,0]),'final_inventory':float(E[-1,-1]),
78              'negative_energy_violation':nonnegative}
79      near(E[0,0],initial,'inherited initial inventory')
80      require(result['soc_min']>=EMIN-TOL and result['soc_max']<=EMAX+TOL,'SOC limits')
81      require(result['power_max_kw']<=5000+TOL,'power limit')
82      if closed:near(E[:,-1],6000,'daily closure')
83      if terminal is not None:near(E[-1,-1],terminal,'true terminal inventory')
84      return result

86 def releases(a,kind):
87     rel=a['releases'];nd=len(a['days']);require(rel.shape==(nd,4,144),'release shape')
88     require(np.isfinite(rel[:,0]).all(),'midnight contracts missing')
89     near(rel[:,0],a['q'],'original q locked to midnight snapshot')
90     effective=rel[:,0].copy();adjust=kind in ['q3','q4_3']
91     for k in range(1,4):
92         require(np.isnan(rel[:,k,:k*36]).all(),'revision affects already delivered slots')
93         if adjust:
94              require(np.isfinite(rel[:,k,k*36:]).all(),'missing permitted revision')
95              require(rel[:,k,k*36:].min()>=-TOL,'negative revised quantity')
96              effective[:,k*36:]=rel[:,k,k*36:]
97         else:require(np.isnan(rel[:,k]).all(),'Q2-type unauthorized revision')
98     err=near(effective,a['r'],'latest legal release reconstructs final r')
99     near(a['r'][:,:36],a['q'][:,:36],'first six hours locked')
100    return {'passed':True,'final_r_reconstruction_error':err,'official_revision_nodes_per_day':4 if adjust else 1,
101             'meaning':'r is delivery-wise final effective quantity; every original q equals its daily 00:00
               ,→ snapshot.'}
102
103 def compare_reported_ledger(ledger,reported_daily,reported_totals,dates,E):
104    require(len(reported_daily)==len(dates),'daily record length')
105    maxerr=0.
106    for i,row in enumerate(reported_daily):
107         require(str(row['date'])==str(dates[i]),'daily report date')
108         for key,value in ledger.items():maxerr=max(maxerr,near(row[key],value[i],f'daily {key}'))
109         maxerr=max(maxerr,near(row['ending_inventory'],E[i,-1],'daily ending inventory'))
110    if reported_totals is not None:
111         for key,value in ledger.items():maxerr=max(maxerr,near(reported_totals[key],np.sum(value),f'annual
           ,→ {key}'))
112    return maxerr

114 def model_metadata(kind,cutoff,days):
115    suffix='' if days==3 else f'_D{days}'
116    relative=f'artifacts/tails/{kind}_{cutoff}{suffix}.json'
117    if relative not in MODEL_CACHE:
118         path=ROOT/relative
119         if not path.exists():raise Pending('Missing used tail '+relative)
120         m=json.loads(path.read_text());cfg=m['configuration']
121         require(cfg['cutoff_day_exclusive']==cutoff,'tail cutoff metadata')
122         require(cfg['horizon_days']==days and cfg['steps_per_day']==12,'tail horizon/granularity')
123         require(cfg['history_days']==list(range(max(0,cutoff-28),cutoff)),'tail mature complete-day window')
124         require(cfg['variable_price']==kind.startswith('q4'),'tail price setting')
125         require(cfg['adjustments']==(kind in ['q3','q4_3']),'tail adjustment setting')
126         require(m['iterations']==500,'tail iterations differ from frozen development/evaluation budget')
127         cuts=np.asarray(m['root_cuts']);require(cuts.ndim==2 and cuts.shape[1]==2 and
           ,→ np.isfinite(cuts).all(),'one-dimensional tail cuts')




                                                           79
128        MODEL_CACHE[relative]={'sha256':sha(path),'configuration':cfg,'cut_count':len(cuts),'iterations':m['itera ⌋
           ,→  tions']}
129    return relative,MODEL_CACHE[relative]

131 def information(a,meta,kind,candidate,config,allowed=None):
132    rows=meta['decisions'];require(len(rows)==len(a['days'])*4,'four planning nodes per day')
133    closed=bool(config['closed_daily']);official=kind in ['q3','q4_3'];sddp=candidate.startswith('sddp')
134    horizon=int(config['horizon_days']);count=int(config['count']);scale=float(config['tail_scale'])
135    used=set();slack=[];tail_hist={};maxinv=0.;cover={}
136    for i,day in enumerate(a['days']):
137        for k in range(4):
138             row=rows[i*4+k];now=int(day)*144+k*36;end=min((int(day)+horizon)*144,TEND);length=end-now
139             require(row['as_of']==now and row['release']==k and row['end']==end,'planning absolute clock')
140             require(row['date']==str(a['dates'][i]),'planning date')
141             maxinv=max(maxinv,near(row['inventory'],a['state'][i,k*36],'planning input SOC'))
142             # Derive all matured historical issues first, then use the most
143             # recent 28; this is independent of the producer's search shortcut.
144             historical=[h for h in range(7,int(day)) if h*144+k*36+length<=now][-28:]
145             if count and len(historical)>count:
146                  positions=np.rint(np.linspace(0,len(historical)-1,count)).astype(int)
147                  historical=[historical[j] for j in positions]
148             require(row['history_days']==historical,'mature residual block selection')
149             last=max(historical)*144+k*36+length
150             require(row['latest_training_target_exclusive']==last and last<=now,'last actual training target')
151             slack.append(now-last)
152             if official:
153                  nodes=range(4) if allowed is None else allowed
154                  issue=max(d*144+j*36 for d in [int(day)-1,int(day)] for j in nodes if d*144+j*36<=now)
155                  covered=max(0,min(end,issue+144)-now)
156             else:covered=0
157             require(row['official_covered_slots']==covered,'already released full-24h coverage')
158             cover[str(covered)]=cover.get(str(covered),0)+1
159             use_tail=sddp and not closed and end<TEND
160             if use_tail:
161                  month=a['dates'][i].astype('datetime64[M]').astype('datetime64[D]')
162                  cutoff=int((month-np.datetime64('2025-01-01')).astype(int))
163                  D=min(3,(TEND-end)//144)
164                  require(D>=1 and row['tail_model_cutoff']==cutoff and row.get('tail_model_days')==D,'as-of month
                    ,→ or remaining-day tail mismatch')
165                  path,model=model_metadata(kind,cutoff,D);used.add(path)
166                  require(1<=row['tail_cut_count']<=model['cut_count']+1,'active cut count')
167                  near(row['tail_price'],0,'SDDP replaces linear tail',0)
168                  tail_hist[str(D)]=tail_hist.get(str(D),0)+1
169             else:
170                  require(row['tail_model_cutoff'] is None and row['tail_cut_count']==0 and
                    ,→ row.get('tail_model_days') is None,'unexpected tail model')
171                  if closed or end==TEND:near(row['tail_price'],0,'true endpoint or constant closed tail',0)
172                  elif not kind.startswith('q4'):
173                       near(row['tail_price'],scale*float(np.quantile(DATA['day_price'],.25))/ETA,'fixed-price linear
                         ,→ tail',1e-10)
174                  else:require(np.isfinite(row['tail_price']) and row['tail_price']>=0,'variable-price linear tail
                    ,→ finite')
175    return {'passed':True,'decisions':len(rows),'minimum_training_maturity_slack_slots':int(min(slack)),
176             'input_SOC_max_error':maxinv,'official_coverage_histogram':cover,
177             'tail_days_histogram':tail_hist,'used_model_files':sorted(used),
178             'scope_limit':'Checks recorded time/index permissions and file metadata. Nonanticipativity of the
               ,→ producing algorithm additionally relies on separately executed source/mutation reviews; it cannot
               ,→ be proved from one realized trajectory alone.'}

180 def ordinary_case(stem,kind,candidate,allowed=None):
181     a,m,hs=read_pair(stem)
182     near(a['days'],DAYS,'full annual day indices',0)
183     near(a['dates'].astype('datetime64[D]').astype(int),DATES.astype(int),'full annual calendar',0)
184     require(m['kind']==kind and m['candidate']==candidate,'method identity')
185     cfg=m['configuration']
186     require(cfg['start_day']==31 and cfg['end_day']==364 and cfg['final'] is None,'main evaluation endpoints')
187     require(cfg['count']==7 and cfg['horizon_days']==2 and cfg['tail_scale']==1 and cfg['grid']==321,'matched
       ,→ production configuration')
188     require(cfg['closed_daily']==candidate.startswith('closed_'),'daily closure configuration')




                                                            80
189    require(cfg.get('initial',6000)==6000,'annual initial inventory configuration')
190    if allowed is not None:
191         design=m['validation_design']
192         require(design['kind']=='q3' and design['start']==31 and design['stop']==365 and
           ,→ design['releases']==allowed,'release-ablation design')
193         require(not design.get('stress',False),'annual ablation must not change actual data')
194    phys=physical(a,kind,6000,closed=cfg['closed_daily']);rel=releases(a,kind);ledger=daily_ledger(a)
195    fee_error=compare_reported_ledger(ledger,m['daily'],m['totals'],a['dates'],a['state'])
196    for key,value in phys.items():near(m['validation'][key],value,'reported physical '+key)
197    info=information(a,m,kind,candidate,cfg,allowed)
198    return {'status':'pass','kind':kind,'candidate':candidate,'files':hs,'days':len(a['days']),
199              'independent_totals':{k:float(v.sum()) for k,v in ledger.items()},
200              'daily_and_annual_cost_reconciliation_max_abs':fee_error,'physical':phys,'releases':rel,'information' ⌋
                ,→ :info}

202 def terminal_case(stem,kind,candidate):
203     a,m,hs=read_pair(stem);source=f'artifacts/annual/{kind}_{candidate}'
204     old,om,oh=read_pair(source)
205     require(m['kind']==kind and m['candidate']==candidate,'terminal case identity')
206     require(m['source_trajectory_sha256']==oh[source+'.npz'],'terminal source trajectory hash')
207     for key in
       ,→ ['source_metrics_sha256','fixed_suffix_configuration','fixed_suffix_daily','fixed_suffix_decisions']:
208         if key not in m:raise Pending('Missing terminal suffix audit evidence '+key)
209     require(m['source_metrics_sha256']==oh[source+'.json'],'terminal source metrics hash')
210     require(m['prefix_days']==332 and m['replayed_days']==[363,364],'terminal splice boundary')
211     near(a['days'],DAYS,'terminal annual days',0)
212     near(a['dates'].astype('datetime64[D]').astype(int),DATES.astype(int),'terminal annual calendar',0)
213     require(set(a)==set(old),'spliced array columns')
214     prefix_error=0.
215     for key in a:
216         if np.issubdtype(a[key].dtype,np.number):
217              aa=a[key][:332];bb=old[key][:332]
218              require(np.array_equal(np.isnan(aa),np.isnan(bb)),'prefix NaN masks '+key)
219              prefix_error=max(prefix_error,near(np.nan_to_num(aa),np.nan_to_num(bb),'unchanged prefix '+key,0))
220         else:require(np.array_equal(a[key][:332],old[key][:332]),'unchanged prefix '+key)
221     phys=physical(a,kind,6000,terminal=6000);rel=releases(a,kind);ledger=daily_ledger(a)
222     source_ledger=daily_ledger(old)
223     near(m['free_total_cost'],source_ledger['total_cost'].sum(),'independent original free cost')
224     near(m['fixed_total_cost'],ledger['total_cost'].sum(),'independent fixed cost')
225     near(m['terminal_constraint_increment'],ledger['total_cost'].sum()-source_ledger['total_cost'].sum(),'terminal
       ,→ cost difference')
226     require(max(m['free_suffix_replay_differences'].values())<=TOL,'producer reported free suffix replay failed')
227     suffix={k:v[332:] for k,v in a.items()};cfg=m['fixed_suffix_configuration'];original=om['configuration']
228     for key in ['count','horizon_days','tail_scale','grid']:require(cfg[key]==original[key],'fixed suffix
       ,→ configuration '+key)
229     require(cfg['start_day']==363 and cfg['end_day']==364 and cfg['final']==6000 and not
       ,→ cfg['closed_daily'],'suffix endpoints')
230     near(cfg['initial'],old['state'][332,0],'suffix initial configuration')
231     near(m['suffix_initial_inventory'],old['state'][332,0],'reported inherited SOC')
232     near(suffix['state'][0,0],old['state'][331,-1],'physical splice SOC')
233     sp=physical(suffix,kind,float(old['state'][332,0]),terminal=6000)
234     for key,value in sp.items():near(m['fixed_suffix_validation'][key],value,'suffix reported physical '+key)
235     sl=daily_ledger(suffix)
236     fee_error=compare_reported_ledger(sl,m['fixed_suffix_daily'],None,suffix['dates'],suffix['state'])
237     info=information(suffix,{'decisions':m['fixed_suffix_decisions']},kind,candidate,cfg)
238     prefix_info=information({k:v[:332] for k,v in old.items()},
239                              {'decisions':om['decisions'][:332*4]},kind,candidate,original)
240     near(ledger['total_cost'].sum(),source_ledger['total_cost'][:332].sum()+sl['total_cost'].sum(),'prefix plus
       ,→ fixed suffix fee')
241     return {'status':'pass','kind':kind,'candidate':candidate,'files':hs,'source_files':oh,'days':334,
242              'independent_totals':{k:float(v.sum()) for k,v in ledger.items()},'physical':phys,'releases':rel,
243              'prefix_exact_max_error':prefix_error,'suffix_daily_cost_reconciliation_max_abs':fee_error,
244              'information':{'prefix':prefix_info,'fixed_suffix':info},
245              'replay_scope':'Independently verifies stored source hashes, every reused prefix element, splice SOC,
                ,→ suffix logs/fees and physical constraints. Does not rerun all eight controllers; prior v2 review
                ,→ independently replayed both Q2 linear-tail cases.'}

247 def main():
248    begin=time.perf_counter();expected=[]

249         for kind in KINDS:

           ,→ ['affine_mpc','markov_mpc']]

                    ,→ score, or final project acceptance.',

276                  'formula_independence':'No production module imported. Bus balance, battery recurrence, fee piecewise
                    ,→ identity, daily/annual aggregation, revision replay, calendar/maturity, tail-file calendar
                    ,→ routing, and prefix splice checks are independent NumPy calculations in this file.',
277                  'reused_evidence':reused,
278                  'reuse_limitations':'Source nonanticipativity, future-data mutation, LP/SDDP dual validity, and
                    ,→ deterministic fixed-suffix policy reproduction are covered by named earlier reviews. This script
                    ,→ does not pretend that recorded timestamps alone prove those algorithmic properties and does not
                    ,→ re-solve optimization models.',

                    ,→ (ROOT/'src').glob('*.py')},
281                  'execution':{'command':'python3 review/check-production.py'+(' --require-complete' if
                    ,→ '--require-complete' in sys.argv else ''),

                                  ,→ ':np.__version__,

                                  ,→ formulas; no sampling.'}}
284         OUT.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n')
285         note=f'''# 生产轨迹独立审计
286
287 审查人 `/root/independent_review`。本次快照：应有36组，已通过{report['passed_cases']}组，待补{len(pending)}组，
      ,→   失败{len(failed)}组；状态 `{status}`。详细逐组结论、原文件哈希与缺项见production-audit.json。这里不作候选排名、
      ,→   模拟评分或最终项目验收。

289 运行 `python3 review/check-production.py` 更新现有产物快照。最终运行 `python3 review/check-production.py
      ,→   --require-complete`：缺项退出2，实质检查失败退出1，仅全部36组通过才退出0。

291 目标为20个普通年度、4个SDDP年度、4个全年预报消融、8个全局期末6000对照。每条年度轨迹覆盖334日、48,096个10分钟段、
      ,→   1336个重规划节点。
292
293 本脚本不导入任何生产模型模块。费用用两种等价分段表达式独立重算，并逐日及全年核对；能量平衡、SOC递推/边界、功率、互斥、
      ,→   午夜连续与真实末端直接由数组检验。原q必须等于0点发布快照，最终r由每段最新合法发布重建；已交付段不能调约，
      ,→   Q2不得有日内调约，头6小时r=q。
294
295 记录中的as-of、发布时点、当前库存、完整24小时预报覆盖、成熟连续历史块均重新按绝对时刻核算。SDDP检查当前月份、
      ,→   剩余D1/D2/D3、H=T零末值及实际被引用模型的训练截止/配置和哈希。全局期末对照另检查源NPZ与源JSON双哈希、
      ,→   全部332日前缀数组逐值一致、继承库存、固定后缀日志/费用和拼接。
296




                                                                82
297 复用的既有证据在JSON中逐项列出并哈希：算法非预见性与未来扰动、独立DP/LP比较、SDDP小树及对偶割、v2后缀实际复演。
      ,→   它们没有在本脚本中重跑；仅凭一条实测轨迹及其时间戳本身，无法证明生产程序没有读取不可见信息。
      ,→   本脚本也不从保存的NPZ声称重新验证了所有上层训练LP最优性。

299        (ROOT/'review/production-audit.md').write_text(note)

      review/check-sddp.py
 1 """Independent extensive-form LP and exhaustive policy audit of finite SDDP."""
 2 from pathlib import Path
 3 import sys,json,hashlib

 5 from scipy.optimize import linprog
 6 ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

10 K=4;ETA=.9;M=5000*24/K;D=1+2*K
11 NET=[[0.,2000.],[1000.,3000.],[-500.,1000.],[800.,2500.]]
12 PRICES=[.2,1.3,.4,1.8]
13 INITIAL=np.r_[6000.,np.zeros(2*K)]

15 def specification(adjust):
16         spec=[('sign',0)]
17         for k in range(K):
18             if adjust and k:spec.append(('revise',k))
19             spec.append(('deliver',k))
20         return spec
21
22 def extensive(spec,state,start=0,clairvoyant=False):
23         """Separate bus c/d variables and direct tree recursion, no producer matrices.
24
25         A variable is a dict {index:1}; scalar constants represent fixed state.
26         Signing/revision controls are created BEFORE the next outcome branches.
27         """
28         bounds=[];cost=[];equal=[];rhs=[];upper=[];ub=[]
29         def var(bound=(0,None),weight=0.):
30             j=len(bounds);bounds.append(bound);cost.append(weight);return {j:1.}
31         def constraint(terms,right,equality=False):
32             row={};constant=0.
33             for weight,expr in terms:
34                 if isinstance(expr,dict):
35                     for j,v in expr.items():row[j]=row.get(j,0.)+weight*v
36                 else:constant+=weight*float(expr)
37             (equal if equality else upper).append(row);(rhs if equality else ub).append(right-constant)
38         def visit(t,E,q,r,prob):
39             if t==len(spec):return
40             kind,hour=spec[t]
41             if kind=='sign':
42                 newq=[var() for _ in range(K)]
43                 visit(t+1,E,newq,newq.copy(),prob)
44             elif kind=='revise':
45                 newr=[r[k] if k<hour else var() for k in range(K)]
46                 visit(t+1,E,q,newr,prob)
47             else:
48                 for demand in NET[hour]:
49                     node_prob=prob/2;price=PRICES[hour]
50                     c=var((0,M));d=var((0,M));em=var(weight=node_prob*5*price)
51                     bill=var(weight=node_prob*price);end=var((1200,10800))
52                     constraint([(1,end),(-1,E),(-ETA,c),(1/ETA,d)],0,True)
53                     constraint([(-1,r[hour]),(-1,d),(-1,em),(1,c)],-demand)
54                     constraint([(1.5,r[hour]),(-.5,q[hour]),(-1,bill)],0)
55                     constraint([(.5,r[hour]),(.5,q[hour]),(-1,bill)],0)



                                                              83
56                 qq=q.copy();rr=r.copy();qq[hour]=0.;rr[hour]=0.
57                 visit(t+1,end,qq,rr,node_prob)
58     visit(start,float(state[0]),list(map(float,state[1:1+K])),list(map(float,state[1+K:])),1.)
59     def matrix(rows):
60         a=np.zeros((len(rows),len(bounds)))
61         for i,row in enumerate(rows):
62             for j,v in row.items():a[i,j]=v
63         return a
64     res=linprog(cost,A_ub=matrix(upper),b_ub=ub,A_eq=matrix(equal),b_eq=rhs,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
65     assert res.success,res.message
66     return float(res.fun)
67
68 def policy_value(model,state,t=0):
69     if t==len(model.stages):return 0.
70     stage=model.stages[t];total=0.
71     for k,prob in enumerate(stage.probabilities):
72         sol=stage.solve(state,k,model.cuts[t+1])
73         total+=prob*(sol.stage_cost+policy_value(model,sol.state,t+1))
74     return float(total)
75
76 def one_case(adjust):
77     spec=specification(adjust)
78     stages=[sddp.battery_stage(K,kind,h,NET[h],[PRICES[h]]*2,[.5,.5]) for kind,h in spec]
79     exact=extensive(spec,INITIAL);model=sddp.SDDP(stages,seed=2121+int(adjust))
80     def explore(i,x):x[0]=[1200.,3600.,6000.,8400.,10800.][i%5];return x
81     batches=[]
82     for batch in range(5):
83          model.train(INITIAL,iterations=100,exploration=explore)
84          lower=model.bound(INITIAL);policy=policy_value(model,INITIAL)
85          assert lower<=exact+1e-5 and policy>=exact-1e-5
86          batches.append({'iterations':(batch+1)*100,'lower_bound':lower,'exact_tree_cost':exact,'exact_policy_cost ⌋
           ,→ ':policy})
87          if policy-lower<1e-5:break
88     assert policy-lower<1e-5,('SDDP not converged on toy',adjust,batches)
89     # Check every cut at independently valued state probes at every stage.
90     probes=[[] for _ in stages]
91     for E in [1200.,3600.,6000.,8400.,10800.]:
92          x=INITIAL.copy();x[0]=E;inputs,_=model.forward(x)
93          for t,state in enumerate(inputs):probes[t].append(state.copy())
94     # Include nonzero q/r states so both refund and increase branches are tested.
95     for t,(kind,h) in enumerate(spec):
96          if kind=='sign':continue
97          for qval,rval in [(600.,300.),(600.,900.)]:
98              x=INITIAL.copy();x[1+h:1+K]=qval;x[1+K+h:]=rval;probes[t].append(x)
99     cut_max=-np.inf;probe_count=0;upper_max=-np.inf
100    for t,states in enumerate(probes):
101         for x in states:
102             truth=extensive(spec,x,t);cuts=model.cuts[t]
103             cut_max=max(cut_max,max(a+b@x-truth for a,b in cuts));probe_count+=1
104             assert max(a+b@x for a,b in cuts)<=truth+1e-5
105    # Root signing discards fully delivered contracts, so root gradients vanish.
106    root_qr=max(np.abs(b[1:]).max() for _,b in model.cuts[0]);assert root_qr<1e-7
107    # Deterministic one-delivery accounting: q100,r80 ->90; q100,r120 ->130.
108    fee_checks=[]
109    delivery=sddp.battery_stage(K,'deliver',0,[0.],[1.],[1.])
110    for qval,rval,want in [(100.,80.,90.),(100.,120.,130.)]:
111         x=INITIAL.copy();x[1]=qval;x[1+K]=rval
112         sol=delivery.solve(x,0,[]);assert abs(sol.stage_cost-want)<1e-7
113         assert abs(sol.state[1])+abs(sol.state[1+K])<1e-8
114         fee_checks.append({'q':qval,'r':rval,'expected':want,'actual':sol.stage_cost})
115    return {'adjustments':adjust,'stages':len(stages),'full_path_count':16,'status':'pass',
116             'independent_extensive_cost':exact,'sddp_lower_bound':lower,'enumerated_policy_cost':policy,
117             'bound_policy_gap':policy-lower,'training_batches':batches,'cut_state_probes':probe_count,
118             'all_cut_max_lower_bound_violation':cut_max,'root_unused_contract_gradient_max_abs':root_qr,
119             'one_delivery_fee_checks':fee_checks}
120
121 def main():
122    before=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'src/sddp.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
123    cases=[one_case(False),one_case(True)]

        ,→ with/without pre-noise revisions; exact extensive LP and all-path policy values. Not validation of
        ,→ original-data MPC terminal quality or universal negative-cost models.',

                 ,→ nitial_state':INITIAL.tolist()},
128               'initial_bound_condition':'Every supplied theta lower bound must be a valid remaining-value lower
                 ,→ bound. BatteryStage uses zero because all remaining costs are nonnegative.'}

57 def route_check():
58    class Intercept(Exception):pass
59    original=rr.tail_file;rows=[]
60    def record(kind,cutoff,days=3):
61         rows.append({'kind':kind,'cutoff':cutoff,'days':days});raise Intercept()
62    rr.tail_file=record
63    try:
64         for length,expected in [(3,1),(4,2),(5,3),(8,3)]:
65              try:rr.simulate(DATA,SEL,list(range(58,58+length)),'q2','sddp_mpc',count=3)
66              except Intercept:pass
67              else:raise AssertionError('Expected a tail request')
68              assert rows[-1]=={'kind':'q2','cutoff':31,'days':expected}
69         # H=T: there must be no tail request; nonstandard initial proves it is
70         # inherited even when this standalone replay starts inside the year.
71         a,m=rr.simulate(DATA,SEL,[363,364],'q2','sddp_mpc',count=3,initial=7123.)
72         check(a['state'][0,0],7123)
73         assert all(z['tail_model_days'] is None and z['tail_cut_count']==0 and z['tail_price']==0 for z in
          ,→ m['decisions'])
74    finally:rr.tail_file=original
75    # Numeric reachable-set proof for every prefix action, independent of state.
76    prefix_last_day=362;T=365*144
77    remaining=T-(np.arange(31*144,(prefix_last_day+1)*144))-1
78    lower=6000-remaining*.9*(5000/6);upper=6000+remaining*(5000/6)/.9
79    assert lower.max()<=1200 and upper.min()>=10800
80    return {'passed':True,'intercepted_requests':rows,'actual_zero_tail_decisions':len(m['decisions']),
81              'optional_initial_kwh':7123.,'prefix_last_date':str(DATA['dates'][prefix_last_day]),
82              'minimum_remaining_steps_in_prefix':int(remaining.min()),
83              'steps_sufficient_for_entire_SOC_range':7,
84              'minimum_prefix_reachability_slack_kwh':float(min(1200-lower.max(),upper.min()-10800)),
85              'scope':'Intercepted file requests test routing only. The zero-tail case executes actual original
               ,→ data.'}

                        ,→ ation']['final_inventory']})

              ,→ global end, and exact match to inherited two-day suffix. This does not establish reuse across
              ,→ changed SDDP tail model versions.'}

15 def score(y,p):
16  e=np.asarray(p)-np.asarray(y)
17  return {'n':int(e.size),'MAE':float(abs(e).mean()),'RMSE':float(np.sqrt(np.mean(e*e))),'bias':float(e.mean())}

     revision/prediction-control/gain_sensitivity.py
 1 """January-only post-review sensitivity, NOT retrospective parameter selection.
 2 Original modules stay unchanged. The LP wrapper changes only gain bounds and
 3 records columns identically absent from BOTH constraint matrix and objective.
 4 """
 5 from pathlib import Path
 6 import sys,json,time,hashlib
 7 from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor,as_completed
 8 import numpy as np
 9 HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
10 BASE=HERE.parents[1].parent/'C 题 _ 跨日随机控制'
11 sys.path[:0]=[str(BASE/'src'),str(BASE/'vendor')]

14 def job(kind,bound):
15    start=time.perf_counter();records=[];raw=control.lp_solve
16    def measured(obj,A,b,bounds,solver='ipm'):
17      cols=[j for j,v in enumerate(bounds) if v==(-10,10)];new=list(bounds)
18      for j in cols:new[j]=(-bound,bound)
19      sol=raw(obj,A,b,new,solver);Ac=A.tocsc(copy=True);Ac.eliminate_zeros()
20      null=(np.diff(Ac.indptr)[cols]==0)&(np.asarray(obj)[cols]==0)
21      vals=sol.x[cols];touch=abs(vals)>=bound-1e-6
